Could use L-BFGS-B with penalty:
```
def pen_obj(x,n,alpha,mu):
  c,dx; lse; val, g = lse...
  penalty = (x.sum()-n/2)**2
  return val + 0.5*mu*pen
def pen_grad(x,n,alpha,mu):
  g = lse_grad; g += mu*(x.sum()-n/2)
  return g
```

def range_intervals(tmin,tmax,excl):
   intervals=[]; cur=tmin
   for e in sorted(excl): if e<tmin or e>tmax continue; if e>cur: intervals.append((cur,e-1)); cur=e+1
   if cur<=tmax: intervals.append((cur,tmax))
   return intervals
```

  L=2n-1; off=n-1
  g=np.zeros(n)
  # threshold q > 1e-15

    tj=idx-off
    w=q[idx]
    if tj>=0:
       # p first i=0..n-1-tj: term (1-h[p+t]) to g[p], term -h[p] to g[p+t]

Projection:
```
def project_box_sum(v,n):
    target=n/2.0
    # If v has nan? handle.
    lo=-1.0; hi=2.0? Need sum(clip(v-lambda,0,1)) decreases with lambda.
    # λ bounds such that sum at lo>=target, hi<=target.
    # If λ very negative, clip=1 max n; λ very large,0.
    # min(v)-1, max(v)
    lam_lo = float(np.min(v)) - 1.0
    lam_hi = float(np.max(v)) + 1.0
    # ensure sum at lo >= target? clip(v-lam_lo) >= clip(v-min(v)+1?) v-min(v)≥0, minus? If lam_lo=min-1, v-lam_lo>=1 -> all 1, sum n >= target.
    # lam_hi=max+1, v-lam_hi<=-1 -> all 0.
    for _ in range(60): mid=(lo+hi)/2; s=np.clip(v-mid,0,1).sum(); if s>target: lo=mid else hi=mid
    return np.clip(v-(lo+hi)/2,0,1)
```
After 60, sum within 1e-12. Then adjust exactly? We can make exact by final correction on non-boundaries but might break [0,1]. Since evaluate sum exactly? Constraint says sum exactly. Harness likely checks? We can enforce to 1e-12. Use final iterative: diff=target-sum; if nonzero, distribute to entries with strict interior. But bisection enough double maybe 1e-14. We can do a few Newton updates: For coordinate rounding? We can implement exact float: `h = np.clip(h,0,1); for _ in range(100): diff=target-h.sum(); if abs(diff)<1e-15 break; room = (h>1e-12)&(h<1-1e-12); if diff>0: add diff/room.sum() to room else subtract etc; clip` but scaling can overshoot. Projection enough. Use 80 iterations for safety. n=400, 80*400=32k, okay.

### LSE objective and gradient
Top-level:
```
def lse_objective(x, n, alpha):
    c = np.correlate(x, 1-x, 'full')*(2.0/n)
    m = c.max()
    if alpha == 0: return m
    sh = alpha*(c-m)
    # for numerical stability, mask sh > -745
    sm = sh.max()
    s = np.exp(sh-sm).sum()
    return m + (sm + np.log(s))/alpha
```
Since m already max, sm=0 (if max included). shifted max 0, so obj=m+log(s)/alpha. Good.
`def lse_weights(c, alpha, floor=0.0):`
```
m=c.max(); sh=alpha*(c-m); s=np.exp(sh).sum(); q=exp/s; if floor>0: q=(1-floor)*q+floor/len(c); return q
```
If alpha very large and only max at multiple lags, q sparse. If all sh -inf? max zero included.

Gradient from q:
```
def grad_from_weights(h, q, n, thresh=1e-14):
   dx=2.0/n
   L=2*n-1; off=n-1
   g=np.zeros(n)
   nz = np.where(q > thresh)[0]
   for idx in nz:
      t=idx-off; w=q[idx]
      if t>=0:
         m=n-t
         g[:m] += w * (1.0 - h[t:])
         g[t:n] -= w * h[:m]
      else:
         s=-t; m=n-s
         g[s:n] += w * (1.0 - h[:m])
         g[:m] -= w * h[s:n]
   return dx * g
```
Check sign for negative: earlier g[s:] += w*(1-h[:m]); g[:m] -= w*h[s:]. Good.
For t=0 branch: g += w*(1-h) - w*h = w*(1-2h). Good.

But q computed on c (dx scaled). Gradient of LSE/alpha = q * gradient c. We multiply by dx. Good.

For SLSQP objective with alpha, can call lse_objective and lse_jac:
```
def lse_obj_jac(x,n,alpha):
    c = compute_c(x,n)
    m = c.max(); sh=alpha*(c-m); e=np.exp(sh); q=e/e.sum(); obj=m+np.log(e.sum())/alpha
    g = grad_from_weights(x,q,n)
    return obj,g
```
To avoid recomputing c in obj and jac separately for SLSQP? SLSQP calls fun and jac separately; okay.

Could add tiny uniform q to ensure all lags? `floor=1e-3` maybe. But objective value then not LSE? If we mix q in gradient only, objective still LSE; inconsistent. For gradient consistency, objective should be weighted sum with same q? q depends on h, not linear. Can't mix. Use LSE.

### SLSQP
```
def run_slsqp(h,n,alpha,maxiter,ftol):
   cons=[{'type':'eq','fun': sum_fun, 'jac': sum_jac}] # sum_jac returns ones
   bounds=[(0,1)]*n
   return minimize(lse_objective, h, args=(n,alpha), method='SLSQP', jac=lse_grad, bounds=bounds, constraints=cons, options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':ftol})
```
Top-level `sum_fun(x,n)`, `sum_jac(x,n)` returns np.ones(n).
Potential issue: `minimize` requires jac return same shape; good.
SLSQP with α high and large n: bounds list 400; okay.
Need project after result.

Could use `trust-constr`:
```
def run_trust(h,n,alpha,maxiter):
  res = minimize(lse_objective, h, args=(n,alpha), method='trust-constr', jac=lse_grad, bounds=bounds, constraints=[LinearConstraint(np.ones(n), n/2, n/2)], options={'gtol':1e-8,'xtol':1e-12,'maxiter':maxiter})
```
Need import `from scipy.optimize import minimize, LinearConstraint`.
trust-constr may be slower but handles equality robust. We can use if available.

But SLSQP with linear equality may have trouble if starting not exactly feasible; project first.

### Custom projected gradient
We can implement an efficient `refine_pg(h,n,deadline,best_c,alpha_seq)`:
Pseudo:
```
def pg_refine(h,n,deadline,max_iters=20000,alpha_seq=(50,100,200,500,1000,2000,5000,10000,20000,50000), base_lr=0.5):
  h=project(h,n); c=calc; cmax=c.max(); best=h.copy(); bestc=cmax; bestlse=lse_obj
  for alpha in alpha_seq:
    if time>deadline break
    cmax=c.max(); f=lse_obj(c,alpha)
    lr=0.2? 
    for it in range(max_iters):
       if time>deadline: break
       q=lse_weights(c,alpha)
       g=grad_from_weights(h,q,n)
       gn=np.linalg.norm(g)
       if gn<1e-12: c_recompute; break
       step=lr
       improved=False
       for _ in range(30):
          v=project_box_sum(h - step*g, n)
          v=cmax? c2=calc; c2max=c2.max(); f2=lse_obj(c2,alpha)
          # Armijo based on f
          if f2 < f - 1e-5*step*gn*0.1: # or c2max < cmax - 1e-12
             h=v; c=c2; cmax=c2max; f=f2; step_lr=step; improved=True; break
          step *=0.5
       if not improved:
          # try random restart small or break alpha
          # maybe accept if cmax improved even if LSE not
          v=project(h - 0.01*g); if cmax(v)<bestc: update
          break
    update best if cmax<bestc
```
Line search cost: each trial correlate O(n^2). 30* many. Could be heavy. Use fixed line search with few trials. Since objective smooth, choose step via heuristic: step = 1.0 / (np.linalg.norm(g,inf)*10?) Could use backtracking 5 trials. n=400, correlate fast.

But projected gradient on nonconvex maybe slow to get 1e-5. SLSQP likely better.

Let's set c array index idx from 0 to 2n-2, k = idx - (n-1). Formula:

Thus derivative:
For k>0 (idx s): term h_{i+s} - h_i h_{i+s}. Derivative wrt h_p:
- as first h_{i+s}=p: p=i+s => i=p-s, i≥0 => p=s..n-1. Term derivative (1 - h_i) = (1 - h_{p-s}).
- as second h_i=p: i=p, p+s valid => p=0..n-1-s. Term derivative -h_{i+s} = -h_{p+s}.
So g[p] += w*(1-h_{p-s}) for p=s..n-1; g[p] -= w*h_{p+s} for p=0..n-1-s.
For k=0: g += w*(1-2h).
For k=-s: term h_i(1-h_{i+s}); derivative:
- as first h_i=p: i=p, p+s valid => p=0..n-1-s: derivative (1-h_{p+s}).
- as second h_{i+s}=p: i=p-s, p=s..n-1: derivative -h_{p-s}.
So g[0..n-1-s] += w*(1-h[s:]); g[s..n-1] -= w*h[:-s].
This is symmetric; we can compute using two branches.

Earlier gradient had positive/negative swapped. Need correct. Let's write `grad_from_weights` with this formula:
```
for idx:
 k=idx-off
 if k>0:
  s=k; m=n-s
  # g[s:] += w*(1 - h[:-s])  (p-s=0..m-1)
  g[s:n] += w*(1.0 - h[:m])
  # g[:m] -= w*h[s:]
  g[:m] -= w*h[s:n]
 elif k<0:
  s=-k; m=n-s
  # g[:m] += w*(1 - h[s:])
  g[:m] += w*(1.0 - h[s:n])
  # g[s:] -= w*h[:m]
  g[s:n] -= h[:m]*w
 else:
  g += w*(1-2h)
```
Check with k=0 included. Good.

Now pair delta formulas must match this output. Since c_s and c_-s formulas swapped. Let's derive pair delta with general valid terms by first/second. But the objective set over all k is symmetric: For each unordered pair of indices (p,q) with q=p+s>0, there are two lags: k=s term uses h_q(1-h_p), k=-s term uses h_p(1-h_q). Thus if we update pair i,j, the affected lags and contributions are summed over all terms involving i or j either as first (higher index?) or second. We can use a unified formula: For lag k>0, first index is higher (p+s), second lower. For lag k<0, first lower, second higher. Instead of deriving first/second by p, we can count each ordered pair (a,b) with a as h and b as 1-h? The c index determined by relation.

Let's define output c_k = sum over r valid h_{r+k}(1-h_r). Here r (call v) is the index for 1-h (the "second" factor in formula), and r+k is index for h (first factor). For each k, the pair (u=h index, v=1-h index) satisfies u-v=k. So c_k raw = Σ_{v: u=v+k valid} h_u (1 - h_v). Thus for each unordered distinct pair {p,q} with p>q, there are two output lags:
- k = p-q >0: term h_p(1-h_q) contributes to idx off+(p-q).
- k = q-p <0: term h_q(1-h_p) contributes to idx off-(p-q).
For k=0: self terms h_i(1-h_i).
This is clearer. The array output index for pair (u,v) where u-v=k: idx = off + (u-v). Term = h_u (1-h_v).
So c_raw indexes are difference u - v where u is index with h, v with 1-h. Difference can negative.
Therefore to compute delta for updating h_i,x and h_j,y, we can consider all pairs (u,v) valid (u,v in [0,n-1]) such that u or v in {i,j}. The lag index idx=off+u-v. Delta = new_term - old_term. Term = H_u*(1-H_v). This is symmetric and straightforward. No ranges with "first index" confusion.
We can sum over all u or v in pair. For each candidate x vector, we can accumulate contributions by pair combinations.
Since only i,j changed, affected (u,v) pairs:
1. Self: u=v=i, idx=off. New x(1-x). u=v=j, idx=off. New y(1-y).
2. Cross between i and j:
- u=i, v=j: idx=off+i-j=off-d (d=j-i). Term x(1-y).
- u=j, v=i: idx=off+d. Term y(1-x).
3. u=i, v=r (r≠i,j): idx=off+i-r. Term x(1-h_r).
4. v=i, u=r (r≠i,j): idx=off+r-i. Term h_r(1-x).
5. u=j, v=r (r≠i,j): idx=off+j-r. Term y(1-h_r).
6. v=j, u=r: idx=off+r-j. Term h_r(1-y).
These are six types (plus self/cross). We can build delta by adding contributions for all r, then correct for self/cross? Let's use base-old to new:
For each r not i,j, four linear contributions involving x/y. We can add full ranges including r=i/j then correct, as before but with correct mapping.
Let's derive efficient full additions using difference idx = off + u - v.
For type u=i: for all v=0..n-1, idx=off+i-v. This maps v=n-1 => off+i-n+1, v=0=>off+i. Since i range 0..n-1, indices off+i-n+1 .. off+i, length n. Baseline contribution old x0(1-h_v) -> new x(1-h_v) if v unchanged. Delta base = (x-x0)*(1-h_v) for all v, including v=i self and v=j cross we'll correct.
For type v=i: u=0..n-1, idx=off+u-i. Range off-i .. off+n-1-i, length n. Term h_u(1-x). Delta base = h_u*(1-x - (1-x0)) = h_u*(x0-x). Include u=i self and u=j cross.
Similarly u=j: idx off+j-v range off+j-n+1..off+j, delta (y-y0)*(1-h_v) = (x0-x)*(1-h_v).
v=j: idx off+u-j range off-j..off+n-1-j, delta h_u*(y0-y)=h_u*(x-x0).
If we add all four full ranges, self and cross terms are double/inaccurate because for self u=v=i, both u=i and v=i add (x-x0)*(1-x0) + x0*(x0-x), but actual self delta is x(1-x)-x0(1-x0), not sum of linear approximations (which is (x-x0)(1-x0)+x0(x0-x) = (x-x0)(1-2x0)? Wait compute: (x-a)(1-a)+a(a-x) = (x-a)(1-a-a)= (x-a)(1-2a). Not exact). Cross terms u=i,v=j: u=i full gives (x-x0)*(1-y0); v=j full gives x0? For v=j term h_u(1-y), u=i: x0*(y0-y)=x0*(x-x0). Sum base cross = (x-x0)(1-y0)+x0(x-x0) = (x-x0)(1-y0+x0). Exact x(1-y)-x0(1-y0). Need correct. The u=i and v=j both refer to same pair? Actually u=i, v=j pair is one term; in type u=i includes it; type v=j with u=i includes it as well, double count. So if adding all four full ranges, we double count every pair where both endpoints change (self and cross) but also for self the two formulas correspond to same term (h_i(1-h_i)) both u=i,v=i and v=i,u=i, double. So we need correct by subtracting base contributions for self/cross and adding exact. Equivalent to previous but now idx positions: self idx off; cross idx off-d and off+d.
Thus algorithm:
- delta zeros (m,L).
- Add u=i full: t? range v=0..n-1, idx = off+i-v, i descending? We can add vector over idx order. But maybe easier use slices with v order.
- Add v=i full: u=0..n-1, idx=off+u-i.

  res = minimize(pen_obj, h, args=(n,alpha,mu), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=pen_grad, bounds=bounds, options={'maxiter':200, 'gtol':1e-8})

```

def lse_grad(x,n,alpha): ...
def sum_part(x,n): ...
def sum_grad(x,n): return np.ones(n)
def pen_obj(x,n,alpha,mu):
    val = lse_obj(x,n,alpha)
    diff = x.sum()-n/2
    return val + 0.5*mu*diff*diff
def pen_grad(x,n,alpha,mu):
    g = lse_grad(x,n,alpha)
    diff = x.sum()-n/2
    g = g + mu*diff
    return g
```

- D_u: p range [max(0,k), n-1-min(0,k)?] Actually p-k∈[0,n-1] => p∈[k, k+n-1], intersect [0,n-1]. If k≥0: p=k..n-1; h[p-k]=h[0..n-1-k]. If k<0: p=0..n-1+k; h[p-k]=h[-k..n-1]. Contribution q*(1-h_segment).
- D_v: p+k∈[0,n-1] => p∈[-k, n-1-k], intersect [0,n-1]. If k≥0: p=0..n-1-k; h[p+k]=h[k..n-1]. If k<0: p=-k..n-1; h[p+k]=h[0..n-1+k].
Now compare to earlier branch:
For k>0 s: D_u g[s:] += w*(1-h[:m]); D_v g[:m] -= w*h[s:]. matches.
For k<0 s: p=0..n-1-s: D_u g[:m] += w*(1-h[s:]); D_v g[s:] -= w*h[:m]. matches. Good. So our corrected gradient branch is right.

def place_q(q, n, M):
   out=np.zeros(M)
   off=n-1
   L=2*n-1
   # k = np.arange(-off, off+1)
   # idx = np.where(k>=0, k, M+k)
   k = np.arange(-off, off+1)
   idx = np.empty(L)
   pos = k>=0
   idx[pos]=k[pos]
   idx[~pos]=M+k[~pos]
   out[idx]=q
   return out
```

Implement gradient direct with q and threshold:
```
def grad_from_q(h,q,n,thresh=1e-12):
    dx=2.0/n; off=n-1; L=2*n-1
    nz=np.flatnonzero(q>thresh)
    if len(nz) == L:
       # maybe still loop; no special
    g=np.zeros(n)
    for idx in nz:
      k=idx-off; w=q[idx]
      if k>0:
         s=k; m=n-s
         g[s:n] += w*(1.0 - h[:m])
         g[:m] -= w*h[s:n]
      elif k<0:
         s=-k; m=n-s
         g[:m] += w*(1.0 - h[s:n])
         g[s:n] -= w*h[:m]
      else:
         g += w*(1.0 - 2.0*h)
    return dx*g
```
Check for k=0 branch in loop; if many, `g += w*(1-2h)` each time; if only one idx off. But if q at off threshold, okay. Could handle k=0 before loop or within. Fine.

`lse_obj` and `lse_grad` both compute c and q. To avoid duplicate c in SLSQP? okay.

### LSE objective stable
```
def lse_obj(x,n,alpha):
    c = np.correlate(x,1-x,'full')*(2.0/n)
    m = c.max()
    if alpha <=0: return m
    sh = alpha*(c-m)
    # avoid underflow sum: np.exp(sh) underflows to 0 okay
    s = np.exp(sh).sum()
    return m + np.log(s)/alpha
```
If alpha huge and c-m all -inf? max zero => one exp 1, s≥1.

Weights:
```
def lse_weights(c, alpha, thresh=1e-14):
    m=c.max(); sh=alpha*(c-m); e=np.exp(sh); s=e.sum(); q=e/s; q[q<thresh]=0; if q.sum()>0: q/=q.sum(); else q[off]=1
    return q
```
But zeroing changes sum; renormalize.

def make_pair_xs(lo,hi,x0,n_full=40,n_local=120,local_r=0.1):
    xs1=np.linspace(lo,hi,n_full)

def pair_search(h,c_base,i,j,n,local_r=0.08,ncoarse=32,nlocal=128):
  if i>j: i,j=j,i; x0=h[i]; y0=h[j]; S=x0+y0

  lo...hi...

  # refine around best
  if len(xs)>1:
      a = xs[k-1] if k>0 else xs[k]
      b = xs[k+1] if k<len(xs)-1 else xs[k]
      if b-a < 1e-8: pass

### Pair local search loop
```
def pair_local_search(h, deadline, max_trials=20000, seed=1):
   h=project(h,n); c=compute; cmax=c.max(); best=h.copy(); bestc=cmax
   rng=RandomState(seed)
   n=len(h); dx=2/n
   no_improve=0
   for trial in range(max_trials):
      if time>deadline: break
      # choose random distinct i,j; maybe prioritize pairs with high values? random.
      i=rng.randint(n); j=rng.randint(n)
      if i==j: continue
      # Maybe skip if h values both at same boundary? pair interval may small
      res=pair_search(h,c,i,j,n,local_r=0.05,ncoarse=24,nlocal=96)
      if res is not None:
         x,y,val=res
         h[i]=x; h[j]=y
         # Due to exact sum preserved; project tiny clip
         # recompute c
         c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')*dx
         cmax=c.max()
         if cmax < bestc:
             bestc=cmax; best=h.copy()
         no_improve=0
      else:
         no_improve += 1
         if no_improve > 200: break
   return best,bestc
```
However pair_search recomputes candidate max using c_base; if c_base stale after each accept recompute. Good.

Actually vector 100 candidates =>100k ops, maybe 0.05ms? Python overhead ~0.5ms? 80k*0.5=40s. Could be okay. A full sweep over all pairs sorted by distance? Might find more. But pair_eval for 80k with 100 candidates =8M*100k? 800M element ops maybe tens sec. Maybe within budget. But random 3000 safer.

def lbfgsb_phase(h,n,alpha,mu,maxiter, deadline):
    try:
        res = minimize(pen_obj, h, args=(n,alpha,mu), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=pen_jac, bounds=[(0,1)]*n, options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-15,'gtol':1e-10})

        return h

def slsqp_phase(h,n,alpha,maxiter,deadline):
    h=project_box_sum(h,n)
    cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':sum_fun,'args':(n,),'jac':sum_jac}]
    try:
       res=minimize(lse_obj,h,args=(n,alpha),method='SLSQP',jac=lse_jac,bounds=[(0,1)]*n,constraints=cons,options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-14})

Our branch for k<0: `g[:m] += w*(1.0 - h[s:n])`, `g[s:n] -= w*h[:m]`. correct. For k>0: c_s=Σ h_{i+s}(1-h_i). derivative p as h higher: (1-h_{p-s}), p=s..n-1 => g[s:] +=1-h[:m]; derivative p lower: -h_{p+s}, p=0..m-1 => g[:m]-=h[s:]. correct.

def pair_xs(lo,hi,x0,ncoarse,nlocal,local_r):

```
def lse_weights(c,alpha):
   m=c.max(); sh=alpha*(c-m); e=np.exp(sh); s=e.sum()
   if s==0: q=np.zeros(len(c)); q[np.argmax(c)]=1; return q
   q=e/s; q[q==0]=0; return q
```

### `project_box_sum` details
```
def project_box_sum(v,n):
    target=0.5*n
    v=np.nan_to_num(np.asarray(v,dtype=float), nan=0.0, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
    lo=float(np.min(v)) - 1.0
    hi=float(np.max(v)) + 1.0
    for _ in range(80):
       mid=0.5*(lo+hi)
       s=np.clip(v-mid,0.0,1.0).sum()
       if s>target: lo=mid
       else: hi=mid
    v=np.clip(v-0.5*(lo+hi),0.0,1.0)
    # fix sum
    for _ in range(50):
       diff=target-v.sum()
       if abs(diff)<1e-14: break
       mask=(v>1e-12)&(v<1.0-1e-12)
       cnt=int(mask.sum())
       if cnt==0:
          # distribute uniformly on non-bound? if none, shift all tiny
          if diff>0: v=np.clip(v+diff/n,0,1)
          else: v=np.clip(v+diff/n,0,1)
          continue
       v[mask] += diff/cnt
       v=np.clip(v,0,1)
    return v
```
If `lo=hi` due v constant? min-1 < max+1 yes. Bisection 80 yields lambda. The sum correction may push out but clip; final sum maybe off if cnt=0. If v all 0 or1 and target not at boundary impossible? But target n/2; projection should not all boundary. okay.

Could final exact sum by finding one interior and setting? Enough.

### `compute_c`
```
def compute_c(h,n):
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')*(2.0/n)
```

### `optimize_n` in detail
```
def optimize_n(h,n,seed,deadline):
    h=project_box_sum(h,n)
    c=compute_c(h,n); bestc=float(c.max())
    if bestc < 0: bestc=0
    besth=h.copy(); work=h.copy(); workc=bestc
    if n < 4 or time.time() > deadline: return besth,bestc
    alphas = (20,50,100,200,500,1000,2000)
    mus = (10,50,200,1000,5000,20000,100000)
    for ii in range(len(alphas)):
        if time.time() > deadline - 5: break
        alpha=alphas[ii]; mu=mus[ii]
        maxit = 350 if alpha < 200 else 180
        h2=lbfgsb_phase(work,n,alpha,mu,maxit,deadline)
        h2=project_box_sum(h2,n)
        c2=compute_c(h2,n); v2=float(c2.max())
        if v2 < bestc - 1e-14:
             bestc=v2; besth=h2.copy()
        if v2 <= workc + 1e-7:
             work=h2; workc=v2
        else:
             work=besth.copy(); workc=bestc
    # SLSQP
    for alpha in (500,2000,8000,20000):
        if time.time() > deadline - 8: break
        h2=slsqp_phase(work,n,alpha,220)
        h2=project_box_sum(h2,n)
        c2=compute_c(h2,n); v2=float(c2.max())
        if v2 < bestc:
             bestc=v2; besth=h2.copy(); workc=v2; work=h2
        # Also try from best if work not best? maybe
    # pair
    if time.time() < deadline - 15:
         besth,bestc=pair_local_search(besth, deadline, seed=seed+1)
    # final SLSQP if time
    if time.time() < deadline - 4:
         h2=slsqp_phase(besth,n,50000,300)
         c2=compute_c(h2,n); v2=float(c2.max())
         if v2 < bestc:
             besth=h2.copy(); bestc=v2
    # final project
    besth=project_box_sum(besth,n)
    c2=compute_c(besth,n); bestc=float(c2.max())
    return besth,bestc
```
Potential: SLSQP from `work` that might not be feasible? project. Good.
Pair local search may take long; deadline passed. `pair_local_search` itself should check time and return best. Good.
SLSQP 50000 from best could be expensive; if no time skip.

Maybe L-BFGS `mu` too low initially; but sum exact. Could use alpha sequence and penalty. Good.

### `lbfgsb_phase` needs deadline? It cannot stop inside scipy; maxiter controls. Fine.
```
def lbfgsb_phase(h,n,alpha,mu,maxiter,deadline):
    if n <= 0 or time.time() > deadline: return h
    try:
       bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
       res = minimize(pen_obj, h, args=(n,alpha,mu), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=pen_jac, bounds=bounds, options={'maxiter':maxiter,'maxfun':4*maxiter,'ftol':1e-15,'gtol':1e-9,'disp':0})
       return res.x
    except Exception:
       return h
```
Maybe for n=400, 4*maxiter fun evals 1400, each gradient plus objective; okay.

### `slsqp_phase`
```
def slsqp_phase(h,n,alpha,maxiter):
    h=project_box_sum(h,n)
    if n <=0: return h
    try:
       cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':sum_fun,'args':(n,),'jac':sum_jac}]
       bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
       res=minimize(lse_obj,h,args=(n,alpha),method='SLSQP', jac=lse_jac,bounds=bounds,constraints=cons,options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-14})
       return res.x
    except Exception:
       return h
```
SLSQP may call many; if time long, hmm.

### `interpolate_h`
```
def interpolate_h(h,nold,nnew):
   if nold == nnew: return h.copy()
   x0=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,nold)
   x1=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,nnew)
   return project_box_sum(np.interp(x1,x0,h),nnew)
```
Could avoid project here; optimize_n projects. But okay.

### `pair_local_search` seed
Need not be global. It uses `time`.
```
def pair_local_search(h, deadline, seed=1):
   n=len(h); dx=2.0/n
   h=project_box_sum(h,n); c=compute_c(h,n); bestc=float(c.max()); besth=h.copy()
   rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
   noimp=0; max_no=1200 if n>300 else 2000
   while time.time() < deadline:
      if noimp > max_no: break
      if n <= 1: break
      if rng.random() < 0.85:
         pos=c[n:2*n-1?] # off+1
```
Compute off = n-1. `pos = c[off+1:]` length n-1. `neg = c[off-1::-1]` length n-1. For n=2 off=1, neg=c[0::-1] length1. If n=1, skip.
```
         w = np.maximum(pos, neg) + 1e-12
         w = w / w.sum()
         d = int(rng.choice(n-1, p=w)) + 1
      else:
         d = int(rng.randint(1, n))
      i = int(rng.randint(n-d))
      j = i + d
      res = pair_search(h, c, i, j, n)
      if res is not None:
         x,y,val = res
         h[i]=x; h[j]=y
         c=compute_c(h,n); v=float(c.max())
         if v < bestc:
             bestc=v; besth=h.copy()
         noimp=0
      else:
         noimp += 1
   return besth,bestc
```

### `pair_search` function with candidate xs

```
def pair_search(h,c_base,i,j,n,local_r=0.04,ncoarse=32,nlocal=128):
   if i>j: i,j=j,i
   x0=float(h[i]); y0=float(h[j]); S=x0+y0
   lo=max(0.0,S-1.0); hi=min(1.0,S)
   if hi-lo < 1e-13: return None
   xs=pair_xs(...); 
   vals=pair_eval(h,c_base,i,j,xs,n,S,x0,y0)
   for _ in range(2):
      k=int(np.argmin(vals)); xb=xs[k]
      if k==0: a=xs[0]; b=xs[1]
      elif k==len(xs)-1: a=xs[-2]; b=xs[-1]
      else: a=xs[k-1]; b=xs[k+1]
      if b-a < 1e-10: break
      # optionally clamp width to 0.05 to refine local, but if a,b from coarse and width=0.1, 64 step 0.0015; okay. If first coarse ncoarse=32 width ~0.03; local dense near xold has small intervals. Good.
      xs2=np.linspace(a,b,64)
      vals=pair_eval(...xs2...)
      xs=xs2
   k=int(np.argmin(vals)); bestval=float(vals[k])
   cur=float(c_base.max())
   if bestval < cur - 1e-13:
       return float(xs[k]), float(S-xs[k]), bestval
   return None
```

def pair_xs(lo,hi,x0,ncoarse,nlocal,local_r):
   xs = np.array([lo, hi, x0], dtype=float)
   if hi - lo > 1e-12:
      xs = np.concatenate((xs, np.linspace(lo,hi,ncoarse)))
   a = max(lo, x0 - local_r); b = min(hi, x0 + local_r)
   if b - a > 1e-12:

   xs = np.unique(xs)
   xs = xs[(xs >= lo - 1e-15) & (xs <= hi + 1e-15)]

```

```
def pair_eval(h,c_base,i,j,xs,n,x0,y0,S):
   dx=2.0/n; L=2*n-1; off=n-1; d=j-i; if d<0? pair_search passes i<j.

   exact_self = ...
   delta[:, idx0] += exact_self - base_self
   idxp=off-d
   base_p = ax1*(1-y0) + x0*ax1  # since x0*(x-x0)
   exact_p = xs*(1-yvec) - x0*(1-y0)
   delta[:, idxp] += exact_p - base_p
   idxm=off+d
   base_m = (yvec-y0)*(1-x0) + y0*ax2 # y0*(x0-xs)=y0*ax2
   exact_m = yvec*(1-xs) - y0*(1-x0)
   delta[:, idxm] += exact_m - base_m
```

```
base_self = ax1*(1-x0) + x0*ax2 + ax2*(1-y0) + y0*ax1
```
Exact self: `xs*(1-xs) + yvec*(1-yvec) - (x0*(1-x0)+y0*(1-y0))`.
Good.

Let's draft concise:
```
def project_box_sum(v,n):
    v=np.nan_to_num(np.asarray(v,float)); tgt=n*0.5
    lo=float(v.min())-1; hi=float(v.max())+1
    for _ in range(80):
        mid=(lo+hi)*0.5; s=np.clip(v-mid,0,1).sum()
        if s>tgt: lo=mid
        else: hi=mid
    v=np.clip(v-(lo+hi)*0.5,0,1)
    for _ in range(40):
        d=tgt-v.sum()
        if abs(d)<1e-14: break
        m=(v>1e-12)&(v<1-1e-12); c=int(m.sum())
        if c: v[m]+=d/c
        else: v+=d/n
        v=np.clip(v,0,1)
    return v
```
Good.

`grad_from_q` condense:
```
def grad_from_q(h,q,n):
    g=np.zeros(n); dx=2.0/n; off=n-1
    for idx in np.flatnonzero(q):
       k=idx-off; w=q[idx]
       if k>0:
          s=k; m=n-s
          g[s:n]+=w*(1-h[:m]); g[:m]-=w*h[s:n]
       elif k<0:
          s=-k; m=n-s
          g[:m]+=w*(1-h[s:n]); g[s:n]-=w*h[:m]
       else:
          g+=w*(1-2*h)
    return g*dx
```
Good.

`pair_eval` can be condensed.

Potential issue: `np.flatnonzero(q)` with q dense returns array length 799; loop in Python. Good.

### `optimize_n` detailed:
```
def optimize_n(h,n,seed,deadline):
    h=project_box_sum(h,n)
    c=compute_c(h,n); bestc=float(c.max()); besth=h.copy(); work=h.copy(); workc=bestc
    if n<4 or time.time()>deadline: return besth,bestc
    phases=((10.0,20.0,350),(30.0,100.0,300),(100.0,500.0,250),(300.0,3000.0,200),(1000.0,20000.0,180),(3000.0,100000.0,150))
    for alpha,mu,maxit in phases:
        if time.time()>deadline-6: break
        h2=lbfgsb_phase(work,n,alpha,mu,maxit)
        h2=project_box_sum(h2,n)
        v2=float(compute_c(h2,n).max())
        if v2<bestc: bestc=v2; besth=h2.copy()
        if v2 <= workc + 1e-7: work=h2; workc=v2
        else: work=besth.copy(); workc=bestc
    # pair
    if time.time()<deadline-20:
       besth,bestc=pair_local_search(besth,deadline,seed=seed)
       work=besth.copy(); workc=bestc
    # high L-BFGS after pair
    if time.time()<deadline-10:
       for alpha,mu,maxit in ((3000.0,100000.0,150),(10000.0,500000.0,120)):
          if time.time()>deadline-6: break
          h2=lbfgsb_phase(work,n,alpha,mu,maxit)
          h2=project_box_sum(h2,n); v2=...
          update
    return besth,bestc
```
`lbfgsb_phase` no deadline argument? It can internally check start only. It cannot stop; maxiter limited. Add deadline to check before call.

Potential: `mu` 500000 with n=400, penalty gradient if sum diff 1e-4 -> 50, okay. If diff 0.1 -> 50. good.

def final_polish(h,n,seed,deadline):
   h=project_box_sum(h,n); c=compute_c; besth=h.copy(); bestc=float(c.max())
   # SLSQP
   for alpha,mxi in ((500,150),(2000,120),(8000,100)):
      if time.time()>deadline-8: break
      h2=slsqp_phase(h,n,alpha,mxi); h2=project_box_sum(h2,n)
      v2=float(compute_c(h2,n).max())
      if v2<bestc: besth=h2.copy(); bestc=v2
      h=h2
   # pair
   if time.time()<deadline-20:
      besth,bestc=pair_local_search(besth,deadline,seed=seed+7)
   # one last project/eval
   besth=project_box_sum(besth,n); bestc=float(compute_c(besth,n).max())
   return besth,bestc
```

```

#### `pair_eval` code draft:
```
def pair_eval(h, c_base, i, j, xs, n, x0, y0, S):
    L=2*n-1
    off=n-1
    dx=2.0/n
    omh=1.0-h
    omhrev=omh[::-1]
    m=len(xs)
    delta=np.zeros((m,L), dtype=float)
    ax1=xs-x0
    ax2=x0-xs
    yv=S-xs
    delta[:, i:i+n] += ax1[:,None] * omhrev[None,:]
    si=n-1-i
    delta[:, si:si+n] += ax2[:,None] * h[None,:]
    delta[:, j:j+n] += ax2[:,None] * omhrev[None,:]
    sj=n-1-j
    delta[:, sj:sj+n] += ax1[:,None] * h[None,:]
    idx0=off
    base_self = ax1*(1.0-x0) + x0*ax2 + ax2*(1.0-y0) + y0*ax1
    exact_self = xs*(1.0-xs) + yv*(1.0-yv) - (x0*(1.0-x0) + y0*(1.0-y0))
    delta[:,idx0] += exact_self - base_self
    d=j-i
    idxp=off-d
    base_p = ax1*(1.0-y0) + x0*ax1
    exact_p = xs*(1.0-yv) - x0*(1.0-y0)
    delta[:,idxp] += exact_p - base_p
    idxm=off+d
    base_m = ax2*(1.0-x0) + y0*ax2
    exact_m = yv*(1.0-xs) - y0*(1.0-x0)
    delta[:,idxm] += exact_m - base_m
    return np.max(c_base[None,:] + dx*delta, axis=1)
```
Check `delta[:, i:i+n]` if i+n can exceed L? i max n-1 => i+n=2n-1 ok. `si:si+n`: si=n-1-i, max n-1, +n <=2n-1, min 0+n=n. Good. `sj` similar.
Base self terms: ax2*(1-y0), y0*ax1. Yes.
Base_p: x0*ax1, yes.
Base_m: y0*ax2, yes.
What about if d=0? not.
`c_base[None,:] + dx*delta`: dx float, delta matrix. okay.
`np.max` returns float array length m.

#### `pair_xs`
```
def pair_xs(lo,hi,x0):
    xs=np.array([lo,hi,x0],float)
    if hi-lo>1e-12:
       xs=np.concatenate((xs,np.linspace(lo,hi,32)))
    a=max(lo,x0-0.04); b=min(hi,x0+0.04)
    if b-a>1e-12:
       xs=np.concatenate((xs,np.linspace(a,b,160)))
    xs=np.unique(xs)
    xs=xs[(xs>=lo-1e-12)&(xs<=hi+1e-12)]
    return np.clip(xs,lo,hi)
```
Use fixed local_r=0.04. Good.
If interval very small, xs maybe [lo,hi,x0] duplicates; unique. If `lo==hi`, xs length1.

#### `pair_search`
```
def pair_search(h,c_base,i,j,n):
    x0=float(h[i]); y0=float(h[j]); S=x0+y0
    lo=max(0.0,S-1.0); hi=min(1.0,S)
    if hi-lo<1e-13: return None
    xs=pair_xs(lo,hi,x0)
    vals=pair_eval(h,c_base,i,j,xs,n,x0,y0,S)
    for _ in range(3):
       k=int(np.argmin(vals))
       if len(xs)<=1: break
       if k==0: a=xs[0]; b=xs[1]
       elif k==len(xs)-1: a=xs[-2]; b=xs[-1]
       else: a=xs[k-1]; b=xs[k+1]
       if b-a<1e-11: break
       xs2=np.linspace(a,b,64)
       vals=pair_eval(h,c_base,i,j,xs2,n,x0,y0,S)
       xs=xs2
    k=int(np.argmin(vals)); bv=float(vals[k])
    if bv < float(c_base.max()) - 1e-13:
       xb=float(np.clip(xs[k],lo,hi)); yb=float(S-xb)
       if yb<0.0:
          yb=0.0; xb=S-yb
       elif yb>1.0:
          yb=1.0; xb=S-yb
       return xb,yb,bv
    return None
```
If `len(xs)==1`, `k==0` and len(xs)-1=0; branch k==0 first sets b=xs[1] error. Add if len(xs)<=1 before. done.
Refinement 3 times. If boundary k=0, interval xs[0],xs[1]; next refine boundary maybe same or shrink; after 3, okay. If true min at boundary and xs[0] is lo, evaluating boundary. If `b-a` becomes very small, break.
Potential: `c_base.max()` computed each call; c_base max current. Good.

#### `pair_local_search` active weights c update
```
def pair_local_search(h, deadline, seed=1):
    n=len(h); h=project_box_sum(h,n)
    c=compute_c(h,n); bestc=float(c.max()); besth=h.copy()
    if n<2 or time.time()>deadline: return besth,bestc
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed); noimp=0
    maxno=1200 if n>300 else 2500
    off=n-1
    while time.time() < deadline:
       if noimp>maxno: break
       if rng.random()<0.85:
          pos=c[off+1:]
          neg=c[off-1::-1]
          w=np.maximum(pos,neg)+1e-12
          w=w/w.sum()
          d=int(rng.choice(n-1,p=w))+1
       else:
          d=int(rng.randint(1,n))
       i=int(rng.randint(n-d)); j=i+d
       res=pair_search(h,c,i,j,n)
       if res is not None:
          x,y,_=res
          h[i]=x; h[j]=y
          c=compute_c(h,n); v=float(c.max())
          if v<bestc:
             bestc=v; besth=h.copy()
          noimp=0
       else:
          noimp+=1
    besth=project_box_sum(besth,n); bestc=float(compute_c(besth,n).max())
    return besth,bestc
```
If c length? For n=2 off=1, pos=c[2:] len1, neg=c[0::-1] len1. Good.
`rng.choice` with p length n-1; if n-1=0 no (n<2 returned). Good.
Potential: `pair_search` can be expensive; while loop no max trial but noimp break. Good.

#### `optimize_n` with phases
```
def optimize_n(h,n,seed,deadline):
    h=project_box_sum(h,n)
    c=compute_c(h,n); bestc=float(c.max()); besth=h.copy(); work=h.copy(); workc=bestc
    if n<4 or time.time()>deadline: return besth,bestc
    phases=((10.0,20.0,350),(30.0,100.0,300),(100.0,500.0,250),(300.0,3000.0,200),(1000.0,20000.0,180),(3000.0,100000.0,150))
    for alpha,mu,maxit in phases:
       if time.time()>deadline-6: break
       h2=lbfgsb_phase(work,n,alpha,mu,maxit)
       h2=project_box_sum(h2,n)
       v2=float(compute_c(h2,n).max())
       if v2<bestc:
          bestc=v2; besth=h2.copy()
       if v2 <= workc + 1e-7:
          work=h2; workc=v2
       else:
          work=besth.copy(); workc=bestc
    if time.time()<deadline-20:
       besth,bestc=pair_local_search(besth,deadline,seed=seed)
       work=besth.copy(); workc=bestc
    if time.time()<deadline-12:
       extra=((3000.0,100000.0,150),(10000.0,500000.0,120),(30000.0,2000000.0,100))
       for alpha,mu,maxit in extra:
          if time.time()>deadline-6: break
          h2=lbfgsb_phase(work,n,alpha,mu,maxit)
          h2=project_box_sum(h2,n)
          v2=float(compute_c(h2,n).max())
          if v2<bestc:
             bestc=v2; besth=h2.copy()
          if v2 <= workc + 1e-7:
             work=h2; workc=v2
          else:
             work=besth.copy(); workc=bestc
    besth=project_box_sum(besth,n); bestc=float(compute_c(besth,n).max())
    return besth,bestc
```
This pair search uses deadline; after it may consume to deadline, then extra skipped. But then no high L-BFGS after pair. To ensure high L-BFGS after pair, pair_local_search should stop before deadline? It uses deadline; if it consumes all, no extra. Maybe pass a deadline reduced by 30 for pair:

pair_dl = min(deadline - 30, time.time()+? )
```

    besth,bestc = pair_local_search(besth, pair_dl, seed)
# extra L-BFGS until deadline-10
```

#### `final_polish`
```
def final_polish(h,n,seed,deadline):
   h=project_box_sum(h,n); c=compute_c; besth=h.copy(); bestc=float(c.max()); curh=h; curc=bestc
   for alpha,mxi in ((500,160),(2000,120),(8000,100)):
      if time.time()>deadline-10: break
      h2=slsqp_phase(curh,n,alpha,mxi)
      h2=project_box_sum(h2,n)
      v2=float(compute_c(h2,n).max())
      if v2<bestc:
         bestc=v2; besth=h2.copy()
         curc=v2; curh=h2
      elif v2 <= curc + 1e-7:
         curc=v2; curh=h2
      else:
         curh=besth.copy(); curc=bestc
   if time.time()<deadline-15:
      besth,bestc=pair_local_search(besth,deadline,seed=seed+111)
   besth=project_box_sum(besth,n); bestc=float(compute_c(besth,n).max())
   return besth,bestc
```
If SLSQP raises and returns h unchanged, v2=curc, condition true.

def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):
  t0=time.time(); deadline=t0+budget_s-8
  rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
  try:
    hp=np.array(initial_h_values,dtype=float).copy()
  except Exception:
    n0=400; x=np.linspace(0,2,n0); hp=0.5+0.49*np.sin(3.0*np.pi*x)
    hp=project_box_sum(hp,n0); hp=project_box_sum(hp+0.02*rng.randn(n0),n0)
  n0=len(hp); hp=project_box_sum(hp,n0)
  besth=hp.copy(); bestc=float(compute_c(hp,n0).max()); bestn=n0
  res=[n0]
  for m in (300,256,350,400):
     if m not in res: res.append(m)
  res=[r for r in res if 8 <= r <= 700]
  L=len(res)
  if L==1: fr=(0.85,)
  elif L==2: fr=(0.60,0.20)
  elif L==3: fr=(0.50,0.20,0.10)
  else: fr=(0.45,0.15,0.10,0.10)
  span=max(1.0, deadline-t0); cum=0.0
  for k,nh in enumerate(res):
     if time.time()>deadline: break
     if k == len(res)-1:
        dl_phase=deadline-60
     else:
        cum += fr[k]
        dl_phase=t0+span*min(0.90,cum)-5
     if dl_phase>deadline-60: dl_phase=deadline-60
     if dl_phase<time.time()+10: dl_phase=time.time()+10
     if k==0:
        hinit=besth.copy()
     else:
        hinit=interpolate_h(besth,bestn,nh)
        if k>0:
           hinit=project_box_sum(hinit+0.004*rng.randn(nh),nh)
     hopt,copt=optimize_n(hinit,nh,seed+7*k,dl_phase)
     if copt<bestc:

  if time.time()<deadline-15:

        bestc=cfin; besth=hfin; bestn=hfin? no, bestn same.
  besth=project_box_sum(besth,bestn); bestc=float(compute_c(besth,bestn).max())
  return besth,bestc,bestn

### `interpolate_h` if nold==nnew returns copy; if different. Use centers.
```
def interpolate_h(h,nold,nnew):
    if nold==nnew: return h.copy()
    xo=(np.arange(nold)+0.5)*(2.0/nold)
    xn=(np.arange(nnew)+0.5)*(2.0/nnew)
    return np.interp(xn,xo,h)
```

```python
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize
import time


def compute_c(h, n):
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full") * (2.0 / n)


def project_box_sum(v, n):
    v = np.nan_to_num(np.asarray(v, dtype=float).reshape(-1))
    tgt = 0.5 * n
    lo = float(v.min()) - 1.0
    hi = float(v.max()) + 1.0
    for _ in range(80):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        s = np.clip(v - mid, 0.0, 1.0).sum()
        if s > tgt:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    v = np.clip(v - 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(40):
        d = tgt - v.sum()
        if abs(d) < 1e-14:
            break
        m = (v > 1e-12) & (v < 1.0 - 1e-12)
        c = int(m.sum())
        if c:
            v[m] += d / c
        else:
            v += d / n
        v = np.clip(v, 0.0, 1.0)
    return v


def lse_obj(x, n, alpha):
    c = compute_c(x, n)
    m = float(c.max())
    if alpha <= 0:
        return m
    sh = alpha * (c - m)
    s = float(np.exp(sh).sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        return m
    return m + np.log(s) / alpha


def lse_weights(c, alpha):
    m = float(c.max())
    sh = alpha * (c - m)
    e = np.exp(sh)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        q = np.zeros_like(c)
        q[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return q
    q = e / s
    q[q == 0.0] = 0.0
    r = q.sum()
    if r > 0:
        q /= r
    return q


def lse_grad(x, n, alpha):
    c = compute_c(x, n)
    q = lse_weights(c, alpha)
    g = np.zeros(n)
    dx = 2.0 / n
    off = n - 1
    for idx in np.flatnonzero(q):
        k = idx - off
        w = q[idx]
        if k > 0:
            s = k
            m = n - s
            g[s:n] += w * (1.0 - h_slice_h(x, 0, m))
            g[:m] -= w * h_slice_h(x, s, n)
        elif k < 0:
            s = -k
            m = n - s
            g[:m] += w * (1.0 - h_slice_h(x, s, n))
            g[s:n] -= w * h_slice_h(x, 0, m)
        else:
            g += w * (1.0 - 2.0 * x)
    return dx * g


def h_slice_h(h, a, b):
    return h[a:b]


def sum_fun(x, n):
    return float(x.sum() - 0.5 * n)


def sum_jac(x, n):
    return np.ones(n)


def pen_obj(x, n, alpha, mu):
    d = float(x.sum() - 0.5 * n)
    return lse_obj(x, n, alpha) + 0.5 * mu * d * d


def pen_grad(x, n, alpha, mu):
    d = float(x.sum() - 0.5 * n)
    return lse_grad(x, n, alpha) + mu * d


def lbfgsb_phase(h, n, alpha, mu, maxiter):
    h = project_box_sum(h, n)
    try:
        bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
        res = minimize(pen_obj, h, args=(n, alpha, mu), method="L-BFGS-B",
                       jac=pen_grad, bounds=bounds,
                       options={"maxiter": maxiter, "maxfun": 4 * maxiter,
                                "ftol": 1e-15, "gtol": 1e-9})
        if hasattr(res, "x") and res.x is not None:
            return res.x
    except Exception:
        pass
    return h


def slsqp_phase(h, n, alpha, maxiter):
    h = project_box_sum(h, n)
    try:
        bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
        cons = [{"type": "eq", "fun": sum_fun, "args": (n,), "jac": sum_jac}]
        res = minimize(lse_obj, h, args=(n, alpha), method="SLSQP",
                       jac=lse_grad, bounds=bounds, constraints=cons,
                       options={"maxiter": maxiter, "ftol": 1e-14})
        if hasattr(res, "x") and res.x is not None:
            return res.x
    except Exception:
        pass
    return h


def interpolate_h(h, nold, nnew):
    if nold == nnew:
        return h.copy()
    xo = (np.arange(nold) + 0.5) * (2.0 / nold)
    xn = (np.arange(nnew) + 0.5) * (2.0 / nnew)
    return np.interp(xn, xo, h)


def pair_xs(lo, hi, x0):
    xs = np.array([lo, hi, x0], dtype=float)
    if hi - lo > 1e-12:
        xs = np.concatenate((xs, np.linspace(lo, hi, 24)))
    a = max(lo, x0 - 0.04)
    b = min(hi, x0 + 0.04)
    if b - a > 1e-12:
        xs = np.concatenate((xs, np.linspace(a, b, 96)))
    xs = np.unique(xs)
    xs = xs[(xs >= lo - 1e-12) & (xs <= hi + 1e-12)]
    return np.clip(xs, lo, hi)


def pair_eval(h, c_base, i, j, xs, n, x0, y0, S):
    L = 2 * n - 1
    off = n - 1
    dx = 2.0 / n
    omh = 1.0 - h
    omhrev = omh[::-1]
    delta = np.zeros((len(xs), L), dtype=float)
    ax1 = xs - x0
    ax2 = x0 - xs
    yv = S - xs

    delta[:, i:i + n] += ax1[:, None] * omhrev[None, :]
    si = n - 1 - i
    delta[:, si:si + n] += ax2[:, None] * h[None, :]
    delta[:, j:j + n] += ax2[:, None] * omhrev[None, :]
    sj = n - 1 - j
    delta[:, sj:sj + n] += ax1[:, None] * h[None, :]

    idx0 = off
    base_self = ax1 * (1.0 - x0) + x0 * ax2 + ax2 * (1.0 - y0) + y0 * ax1
    exact_self = xs * (1.0 - xs) + yv * (1.0 - yv) - (x0 * (1.0 - x0) + y0 * (1.0 - y0))
    delta[:, idx0] += exact_self - base_self

    d = j - i
    idxp = off - d
    base_p = ax1 * (1.0 - y0) + x0 * ax1
    exact_p = xs * (1.0 - yv) - x0 * (1.0 - y0)
    delta[:, idxp] += exact_p - base_p

    idxm = off + d
    base_m = ax2 * (1.0 - x0) + y0 * ax2
    exact_m = yv * (1.0 - xs) - y0 * (1.0 - x0)
    delta[:, idxm] += exact_m - base_m

    return np.max(c_base[None, :] + dx * delta, axis=1)


def pair_search(h, c_base, i, j, n):
    if i > j:
        i, j = j, i
    x0 = float(h[i])
    y0 = float(h[j])
    S = x0 + y0
    lo = max(0.0, S - 1.0)
    hi = min(1.0, S)
    if hi - lo < 1e-13:
        return None

    xs = pair_xs(lo, hi, x0)
    vals = pair_eval(h, c_base, i, j, xs, n, x0, y0, S)

    for _ in range(2):
        if len(xs) <= 1:
            break
        k = int(np.argmin(vals))
        if k == 0:
            a, b = xs[0], xs[1]
        elif k == len(xs) - 1:
            a, b = xs[-2], xs[-1]
        else:
            a, b = xs[k - 1], xs[k + 1]
        if b - a < 1e-11:
            break
        xs2 = np.linspace(a, b, 64)
        vals = pair_eval(h, c_base, i, j, xs2, n, x0, y0, S)
        xs = xs2

    k = int(np.argmin(vals))
    bv = float(vals[k])
    if bv < float(c_base.max()) - 1e-13:
        xb = float(np.clip(xs[k], lo, hi))
        yb = float(S - xb)
        if yb < 0.0:
            yb = 0.0
            xb = S - yb
        elif yb > 1.0:
            yb = 1.0
            xb = S - yb
        return xb, yb, bv
    return None


def pair_local_search(h, deadline, seed=1):
    n = len(h)
    h = project_box_sum(h, n)
    c = compute_c(h, n)
    bestc = float(c.max())
    besth = h.copy()
    if n < 2 or time.time() > deadline:
        return project_box_sum(besth, n), float(compute_c(besth, n).max())

    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    noimp = 0
    maxno = 900 if n > 300 else 1800
    off = n - 1

    while time.time() < deadline:
        if noimp > maxno:
            break
        if rng.random() < 0.85:
            pos = c[off + 1:]
            neg = c[off - 1::-1]
            w = np.maximum(pos, neg) + 1e-12
            w = w / w.sum()
            d = int(rng.choice(n - 1, p=w)) + 1
        else:
            d = int(rng.randint(1, n))
        i = int(rng.randint(n - d))
        j = i + d
        res = pair_search(h, c, i, j, n)
        if res is not None:
            x, y, _ = res
            h[i] = x
            h[j] = y
            c = compute_c(h, n)
            v = float(c.max())
            if v < bestc:
                bestc = v
                besth = h.copy()
            noimp = 0
        else:
            noimp += 1

    besth = project_box_sum(besth, n)
    bestc = float(compute_c(besth, n).max())
    return besth, bestc


def optimize_n(h, n, seed, deadline):
    h = project_box_sum(h, n)
    c = compute_c(h, n)
    bestc = float(c.max())
    besth = h.copy()
    work = h.copy()
    workc = bestc

    if n < 4 or time.time() > deadline:
        return project_box_sum(besth, n), float(compute_c(besth, n).max())

    phases = (
        (10.0, 20.0, 350),
        (30.0, 100.0, 300),
        (100.0, 500.0, 250),
        (300.0, 3000.0, 200),
        (1000.0, 20000.0, 180),
        (3000.0, 100000.0, 150),
    )
    for alpha, mu, maxit in phases:
        if time.time() > deadline - 6:
            break
        h2 = lbfgsb_phase(work, n, alpha, mu, maxit)
        h2 = project_box_sum(h2, n)
        v2 = float(compute_c(h2, n).max())
        if v2 < bestc:
            bestc = v2
            besth = h2.copy()
        if v2 <= workc + 1e-7:
            work = h2
            workc = v2
        else:
            work = besth.copy()
            workc = bestc

    pair_dl = deadline - 35
    if time.time() < pair_dl:
        besth, bestc = pair_local_search(besth, pair_dl, seed=seed)
        work = besth.copy()
        workc = bestc

    extra = (
        (3000.0, 100000.0, 150),
        (10000.0, 500000.0, 120),
        (30000.0, 2000000.0, 100),
    )
    for alpha, mu, maxit in extra:
        if time.time() > deadline - 6:
            break
        h2 = lbfgsb_phase(work, n, alpha, mu, maxit)
        h2 = project_box_sum(h2, n)
        v2 = float(compute_c(h2, n).max())
        if v2 < bestc:
            bestc = v2
            besth = h2.copy()
        if v2 <= workc + 1e-7:
            work = h2
            workc = v2
        else:
            work = besth.copy()
            workc = bestc

    besth = project_box_sum(besth, n)
    bestc = float(compute_c(besth, n).max())
    return besth, bestc


def final_polish(h, n, seed, deadline):
    h = project_box_sum(h, n)
    c = compute_c(h, n)
    bestc = float(c.max())
    besth = h.copy()
    curh = h.copy()
    curc = bestc

    for alpha, maxit in ((500, 160), (2000, 120), (8000, 100)):
        if time.time() > deadline - 10:
            break
        h2 = slsqp_phase(curh, n, alpha, maxit)
        h2 = project_box_sum(h2, n)
        v2 = float(compute_c(h2, n).max())
        if v2 < bestc:
            bestc = v2
            besth = h2.copy()
            curc = v2
            curh = h2
        elif v2 <= curc + 1e-7:
            curc = v2
            curh = h2
        else:
            curc = bestc
            curh = besth.copy()

    if time.time() < deadline - 15:
        besth, bestc = pair_local_search(besth, deadline, seed=seed + 111)

    besth = project_box_sum(besth, n)
    bestc = float(compute_c(besth, n).max())
    return besth, bestc


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    deadline = t0 + budget_s - 8
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    try:
        hp = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        n0 = len(hp)
    except Exception:
        n0 = 400
        x = (np.arange(n0) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n0)
        hp = 0.5 + 0.49 * np.sin(3.0 * np.pi * x)
        hp = project_box_sum(hp + 0.02 * rng.randn(n0), n0)

    hp = project_box_sum(hp, n0)
    besth = hp.copy()
    bestc = float(compute_c(hp, n0).max())
    bestn = n0

    res = [n0]
    for m in (300, 256, 350, 400):
        if m not in res:
            res.append(m)
    res2 = []
    for r in res:
        if 8 <= r <= 700:
            res2.append(r)
    res = res2

    L = len(res)
    fr = []
    if L == 1:
        fr.append(0.85)
    else:
        fr.append(0.45)
        rem = (0.80 - 0.45) / float(L - 1)
        for _ in range(L - 1):
            fr.append(rem)

    span = max(1.0, deadline - t0)
    cum = 0.0

    for k, nh in enumerate(res):
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        cum += fr[k]
        dl_phase = t0 + span * min(0.95, cum) - 5
        if dl_phase > deadline - 15:
            dl_phase = deadline - 15
        if dl_phase < time.time() + 10:
            dl_phase = min(time.time() + 10, deadline - 5)
        if dl_phase <= time.time():
            continue

        if k == 0:
            hinit = besth.copy()
        else:
            hinit = interpolate_h(besth, bestn, nh)
            hinit = project_box_sum(hinit + 0.004 * rng.randn(nh), nh)

        hopt, copt = optimize_n(hinit, nh, seed + 7 * k, dl_phase)
        if copt < bestc:
            bestc = copt
            besth = hopt.copy()
            bestn = nh

    if time.time() < deadline - 15:
        hfin, cfin = final_polish(besth, bestn, seed + 999, deadline)
        if cfin < bestc:
            bestc = cfin
            besth = hfin.copy()

    besth = project_box_sum(besth, bestn)
    bestc = float(compute_c(besth, bestn).max())
    return besth, bestc, bestn
```
